Theorem 67.1src/Lib/Cohomology/Etale/ConnectedComponents.lean: IsTest.isRadical_span_idempotent
Let $A$ be a commutative ring such that $\IsTestMACRoBOW {A}$. Let $e \in A$ such that $e$ is an idempotent element. Then the span of $\SingletonsingletonMACRoEW {e}$ is a radical.
Proof.
By 2 lemmas of Mathlib IsIdempotentElem.ker_toSpanSingleton_one_sub_eq_span, mul_pow
. ∎
Theorem 67.2src/Lib/Cohomology/Etale/ConnectedComponents.lean: hasPartitions_locConstCoeff
Let $\Lambda$ be an abelian group. Then $\HasPartitionsMACRoAYH {\testInclMACRoEWI}{\locConstCoeffMACRoEEW {\Lambda}}$.
For any abelian group Λ, the coefficient functor on test rings sending A to the locally constant functions Spec A → Λ is additive for partitions along idempotents, in the sense of `HasPartitions`.
Proof. By IsTest.isRadical_span_idempotent, IsTest.quotient
and 10 lemmas of Mathlib PrimeSpectrum.ext, Ideal.mem_span_singleton', Equiv.bijective, Ideal.Quotient.mk_singleton_self, Eq.le, Ideal.ext, RingHom.ext, LocallyConstant.ext, Ideal.mk_ker, Ideal.mem_prod
. ∎
Theorem 67.3src/Lib/Cohomology/Etale/ConnectedComponents.lean: Comparison.coeffCompBij_id_iff_restrict
Let $C$, $C'$ and $D$ be categories. Let $S$ be a commutative ring. Let $\phi$ be a functor of $C'$ and $C$. Let $N$ be a functor of $C$ and the set of additive commutative group categories. Let $N' \in \AbFunctorMACRoFFB {C'}$. Let $\alpha$ be a homomorphism of $N'$ and the composition of $\phi$ and $N$. Let $\Psi$ be a functor of $C$ and $D$. Let $G$ be a functor of $D$ and the set of module categories of $S$. Let $M$ be a functor of $C$ and the set of module categories of $S$. Let $\theta$ be an isomorphism of the composition of $\Psi$ and $G$ and $M$. Then $\CoeffCompBijMACRoQV {\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_CategoryStruct\_\_id} (\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Functor\_\_comp} (\phi , \Psi))}{\alpha}{G}$, if and only if $\ComparisonIsBijectiveMACRoRO {\ComparisonrestrictMACRoSC {\phi}{\moduleCoeffMACRoEHL {M}}}{\alpha}$.
For φ: C' → C, α: N' → N∘φ, Ψ: C → D, G from D to S-modules and G∘Ψ ≅ M: restriction Ext^n(N, G∘Ψ) → Ext^n(N', G∘Ψ∘φ) is bijective for all n iff Ext^n(N, M) → Ext^n(N', M∘φ) is.
Proof. By moduleCoeff, Comparison.isBijective_iff_of_coeff_iso, Comparison.φ. ∎
Definition 67.4src/Lib/Cohomology/Etale/ConnectedComponents.lean: algBaseChangeAdj
Let $k$ and $S$ be sets. Then $\algBaseChangeAdjMACRoDJG$ is an adjunction of $\algBaseChangeMACRoDJF {S}$ and $\restrictAlgMACRoEQS {k}{S}$ defined as $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Adjunction\_\_mkOfHomEquiv}$ applied to $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Adjunction\_\_CoreHomEquiv\_\_mk}$ applied to the function that maps $x_{3}$ and $x_{4}$ to the composition of the homomorphism equivalence and the inverse of the composition of the homomorphism equivalence and the lift equivalence of the set of carriers of $x_{3}$ and the set of carriers of $x_{4}$, $\mathrm{algBaseChangeAdj\_\_\_proof\_3}$ and $\mathrm{algBaseChangeAdj\_\_\_proof\_4}$.
Base change from commutative k-algebras to commutative S-algebras is left adjoint to restriction of scalars.
Definition 67.5src/Lib/Cohomology/Etale/ConnectedComponents.lean: baseChangeTranspose
Let $C$, $k$ and $S$ be sets. Let $X$ be a functor of $C$ and $\CommAlgMACRoFFA {k}$. Let $Y$ be a functor of $C$ and $\CommAlgMACRoFFA {S}$. Then $\baseChangeTransposeMACRoDKP {X}{Y}$ is an equivalence from the set of homomorphisms of the action of the action of the right whiskering of $\CommAlgMACRoFFA {k}$ and $\CommAlgMACRoFFA {S}$ and $\algBaseChangeMACRoDJF {S}$ and $X$ and $Y$ to the set of homomorphisms of $X$ and the action of the action of the right whiskering of $\CommAlgMACRoFFA {S}$ and $\CommAlgMACRoFFA {k}$ and $\restrictAlgMACRoEQS {k}{S}$ and $Y$ defined as the homomorphism equivalence of $X$ and $Y$.
For functors F to k-algebras and G to S-algebras, the adjunction bijection from natural S-algebra maps S ⊗_k F → G to natural k-algebra maps F → G.

Theorem 67.8src/Lib/Cohomology/Etale/ConnectedComponents.lean: PadicTest.isTest
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $c \in \PadicTestMACRoCHG$. Then $\IsTestMACRoBOW {\PadicTestProdRingMACRoCHK {c}}$.
The product ring of a p-adic test c is a test ring: reduced, absolutely integrally closed, and any a, b admit an idempotent e with a ∣ eb and b ∣ (1 − e)a.
Proof. By IsTest.pi. ∎

Theorem 67.15src/Lib/Cohomology/Etale/ConnectedComponents.lean: PadicTest.exists_isIdemQuotient
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $c \in \PadicTestMACRoCHG$. Let $e \in \PadicTestProdRingMACRoCHK {c}$ such that $e$ is an idempotent element and $e \neq 1$. Then there exists an element $d$ of $\PadicTestMACRoCHG$, such that there exists a homomorphism $f$ of $c$ and $d$, such that $\IsIdemQuotientMACRoBJB {\PadicTestringFunctorMACRoCHV}{e}{f}$.
For an idempotent e ≠ 1 in the ring of a p-adic test c, there is a map of tests f: c → d, universal among maps killing e, that is the quotient by the ideal (e).
Proof. By PadicTest.Cell, cellPoint, PadicTest.ringFunctor
and 9 lemmas of Mathlib Ideal.mem_span_singleton', AlgHom.cancel_right, Subtype.surjective_restrict, eq_zero_of_mul_eq_self_left, nonempty_subtype, Function.ne_iff, AlgHom.liftOfSurjective_comp, LE.le.trans, mul_right_eq_self₀
. ∎

Theorem 67.21src/Lib/Cohomology/Etale/ConnectedComponents.lean: Components.isCohomEquiv_of_connected
Let $\Lambda$ be an abelian group. Let $R$ be a commutative ring. Let $X , Y \in \PointFunctorMACRoCLU$ such that $\IsCohomContinuousMACRoBFV {\Lambda}{X}$ and $\IsCohomContinuousMACRoBFV {\Lambda}{Y}$. Let $u$ be a homomorphism of $X$ and $Y$. Let $U$ be a decreasing function from ideals of $R$ to subfunctors of $Y$. Assume that for all ideals $a_{2}$ of $R$, $\IsRelAffineMACRoBMO$. Let $N$ be a function from categories of commutative rings $A_{2}$ and elements of the action of $Y$ and $A_{2}$ to additive submonoids of $R$ such that for all categories of commutative rings $A_{1}$, for all elements $x_{2}$ of the action of $Y$ and $A_{1}$, for all ideals $a_{1}$ of $R$, $x_{2}$ is an element of the object of $U$ and $A_{1}$, if and only if $\mathrm{Submodule\_\_toAddSubmonoid} (a_{1})$ is less than or equal to $N (A_{1} , x_{2})$ and for all categories of commutative rings $A$, for all elements $x_{1}$ of the action of $Y$ and $A$, if $1$ is an element of $N (A , x_{1})$, then the set of carriers of $A$ is a subsingleton. Assume that for all elements $e_{1}$ of $R$, if $e_{1}$ is an idempotent element, then $\IsCohomDenseMACRoBFW {\locConstCoeffMACRoEEW {\Lambda}}{\CategoryTheorySubfunctorwhiskerMACRoQI {(\MaxmaxMACRoEC {U}{U})}{\testInclMACRoEWI}}$. Assume that for all ideals $a \neq \ToptopMACRoF$ of $R$, if for all elements $e$ of $R$, if $e$ is an idempotent element, then $e$ is an element of $a$ or the difference between $1$ and $e$ is an element of $a$, then $\ComponentsMACRoSH {\Lambda}{\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Subfunctor\_\_fromPreimage} (U , u)}$. Then $\ComponentsMACRoSH {\Lambda}{u}$.
Let X, Y have continuous Λ-cohomology and U_a ⊂ Y (a ideals of R) be affine, behave like zero loci, and split densely along idempotents. If u: X → Y is an equivalence over U_a for each proper a containing e or 1 − e for every idempotent e, u is an equivalence.
Proof. By mapOnTests, IsCohomDense.of_preimage_le, IsCohomDense, zorn_injective_and_surjective, IsRelAffine.preimage, IsCohomDense.isCohomEquiv_of_fromPreimage, hasPartitions_locConstCoeff, HasPartitions.eq_zero_of_zero_ring, CategoryTheory.Subfunctor.whisker, HV.mayer_vietoris_injective, IsCohomDense.of_top_le, IsCohomContinuous.isFilteredColimit_antitoneFamily, HV.map_comp

. ∎

Theorem 67.22src/Lib/Cohomology/Hodge/HodgeSite.lean: TotalH.extClass_sign_comm
Let $C$ be a category. Let $F , G \in \AbFunctorMACRoFFB {C}$. Structure 67.26src/Lib/Cohomology/Hodge/HodgeSite.lean: Cocycle

A 1-cocycle from F to G: homomorphisms D(f): F(c) → G(d) for f: c → d, with D(gf) = G(g)D(f) + D(g)F(f).

Definition 67.70src/Lib/Cohomology/Duality/Contraction.lean: degreeTrunc

Then $\degreeTruncMACRoDQM {d}{n}$ is an end of $\Integer$ and $\FinsuppMACRoDN {I}{(\TotalHMACRoDDE {S}{G})}$ defined as $\mathrm{LinearEquiv\_\_conj\_\_app}$ applied to the inverse of the finitely supported function linear equivalence finitely supported function of $\Integer$ and the linear map of the function that maps $i_{2}$ to $\mathrm{DirectSum\_\_lmap} (\lambda x_{6} . \iteMACRoDD {\mathrm{Lt} (x_{6} + d (i_{2}), n)}{\mathrm{LinearMap\_\_id}}{0})$.
The truncation of finitely supported families (w_i) in total Ext(S, G) to total degree below n: in w_i, keep the components of degree e with e + d_i < n.

Theorem 67.81src/Lib/Cohomology/Duality/Contraction.lean: lerayHirsch_bijective_of_repr
Let $A$ and $C$ be categories. Let $G$ be a functor of $A$ and the set of abelian groups. Let $F \in \AbFunctorMACRoFFB {C}$. Let $I$ be a set. Let $d$ be a function from $I$ to $\Natural$. Assume that $I$ is finite. Let $\phi$ be a functor of $C$ and $A$. Let $\theta$ be a function from elements $i$ of $I$ to elements of $\HMACRoAUT {\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Functor\_\_obj} (\restrictAlongMACRoEQT {\phi}, G)}{F}{d (i)}$ such that for all elements $c$ of $A$, $\IsLerayHirschBasisMACRoBKA {\theta}{\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Functor\_\_obj} (\freeAbMACRoDWL , \reprByMACRoEQH {c})}$. Let $S \in \AbFunctorMACRoFFB {A}$. Then $\IsLerayHirschBasisMACRoBKA {\theta}{S}$.
Let θ be a finite family of classes in Ext(G∘φ, F), for a functor φ. If the Leray–Hirsch map `lerayHirsch` is bijective at ℤ[h_c] for every object c of the target of φ, it is bijective at every S. Known result: Leray–Hirsch theorem: a local basis of cohomology classes is a global basis (Ext form).
Proof. By lerayHirsch_mapRange_comp, TotalH.comp, sigma.ι, TotalH.pullbackAlong, freeAb, H.map, sigma, lerayHirsch, CategoryTheory.Abelian.Ext.toTotalH, bijective_lerayHirsch_iff, reprBy, lerayHirsch.devissage, Devissage.bijective_of_sigma_repr, TotalH.map_homClass, H.sigma_bijective
and 5 lemmas of Mathlib DirectSum.ext, Finsupp.ext, Function.Bijective.of_comp_left, Function.Bijective.comp, Function.Bijective.piMap
. ∎
Theorem 67.82src/Lib/Cohomology/Duality/Contraction.lean: surjective_and_dies_of_quotient_sieve

Then if the homomorphism of the map of $F$ and $q$ is surjective, then for all natural numbers $n$, $\HmapMACRoAWA {\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Functor\_\_comp} (\phi , F)}{\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Functor\_\_map} (\freeAbMACRoDWL , \mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Subfunctor\_\_\_u3b9\_} (R))}{n}$ is surjective and for all carriers $x_{4}$ of the action of the composition of $\phi$ and $F$ and $A$, if for all elements $d$ of $C'$, for all homomorphisms $f$ of $A$ and $d$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Subfunctor\_\_obj} (R , d)}{\mathrm{ULift\_\_up} (f)}$, then $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ConcreteCategory\_\_hom\_\_app}$ applied to the map of the composition of $\phi$ and $F$ and $f$ and $x_{4}$ is equal to $0$, then $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ConcreteCategory\_\_hom\_\_app}$ applied to the map of $F$ and $q$ and $x_{4}$ is equal to $0$.

Definition 67.171src/Lib/Cohomology/ProjectiveLine/LineChernClass.lean: projLineSetoid
Let $B$ be a set. The equivalence relation (1 + uv)v' = v(1 + u'v') on pairs (u, v) in B × B: equality of the points (1 + uv : v) of the projective line.

The projective line as a functor on rings: B goes to the set of points (1 + uv : v) with u, v in B, that is, pairs (u, v) modulo `projLineSetoid`.

Theorem 67.177src/Lib/Cohomology/ProjectiveLine/LineChernClass.lean: projLine.exists_unit_smul_unimodularRow

For a pair x = (u, v) in a commutative ring B, the row (1 + uv, v) is a unit of B times the chosen row `projLine.unimodularRow` of the point of P¹(B) represented by x.
Proof. By projLineSetoid, projLine.unimodularRow
and 2 lemmas of Mathlib Setoid.symm', Quotient.mk_out

Theorem 67.178src/Lib/Cohomology/ProjectiveLine/LineChernClass.lean: projLine.smul_unimodularRow_inj

Then $a$ is equal to $b$ and $q$ is equal to $q'$.

. ∎

Theorem 67.196src/Lib/Cohomology/ProjectiveLine/LineChernClass.lean: map_etaClass_eq_zero_of_coboundary

Theorem 67.197src/Lib/Cohomology/ProjectiveLine/LineChernClass.lean: comp_etaClass_eq_zero_of_isUnit

Lib.Cohomology.ProperBaseChange.ValuativeDensity

Structure 67.208src/Lib/Cohomology/ProperBaseChange/ValuativeDensity.lean: IsProperOver
Let $Z$ be a functor of the set of categories of commutative rings and the set of sets. Let $D$ be a category of commutative rings. Let $s$ be a homomorphism of $Z$ and $\reprByMACRoEQH {D}$. Then $\IsProperOverMACRoBMH {s}$ is a proposition.
Z is proper over D: coincidence of two points is cut out by an ideal, and for one a ∈ Z(T) each geometric point sending D into a valuation ring O comes from a through some T → O.
Theorem 67.209src/Lib/Cohomology/ProperBaseChange/ValuativeDensity.lean: IsProperOver.diagonal_closed
Let $Z$ be a functor of the set of categories of commutative rings and the set of sets. Let $D$ and $C$ be categories of commutative rings. Let $s$ be a homomorphism of $Z$ and $\reprByMACRoEQH {D}$ such that $\IsProperOverMACRoBMH {s}$. Let $x$ and $y$ be elements of the action of $Z$ and $C$. Then there exists an ideal $I$ of the set of carriers of $C$, such that for all categories of commutative rings $C'$, for all homomorphisms $f$ of $C$ and $C'$, $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ConcreteCategory\_\_hom\_\_app}$ applied to the map of $Z$ and $f$ and $x$ is equal to $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ConcreteCategory\_\_hom\_\_app}$ applied to the map of $Z$ and $f$ and $y$, if and only if $I$ is less than or equal to the kernel of the underlying ring hom of $f$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Theorem 67.210src/Lib/Cohomology/ProperBaseChange/ValuativeDensity.lean: IsProperOver.valuative_cover
Let $Z$ be a functor of the set of categories of commutative rings and the set of sets. Let $D$ be a category of commutative rings. Then there exists a category of commutative rings $T$, such that there exists an element $a$ of the action of $Z$ and $T$, such that for all sets $K$, for all elements $x$ of $\mathrm{Field} (K)$, if $\mathrm{IsAlgClosed} (K)$, then for all valuation subrings $O$ of $K$, for all elements $z$ of the action of $Z$ and $\mathrm{CommRingCat\_\_of} (K)$, if for all carriers $d$ of $D$, $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ConcreteCategory\_\_hom\_\_app} (\baseRingMapMACRoDKQ {s}{z}, d)$ is an element of $O$, then there exists a homomorphism $u$ of $T$ and $\mathrm{CommRingCat\_\_of} (\SubtypeSet {x_{3}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {O}{x_{3}})})$, such that $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ConcreteCategory\_\_hom\_\_app}$ applied to the map of $Z$ and the composition of $u$ and the commutative ring category of the subtype of $O$ and $a$ is equal to $z$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Theorem 67.211src/Lib/Cohomology/ProperBaseChange/ValuativeDensity.lean: IsProperOver.ext_of_jointly_injective
Let $Z$ be a functor of the set of categories of commutative rings and the set of sets. Let $D$ and $C$ be categories of commutative rings. Let $s$ be a homomorphism of $Z$ and $\reprByMACRoEQH {D}$ such that $\IsProperOverMACRoBMH {s}$. Let $J$ be a set. Let $K$ be a function from elements of $J$ to categories of commutative rings. Let $\pi$ be a function from elements $j_{2}$ of $J$ to homomorphisms of $C$ and $K (j_{2})$ such that for all carriers $c$ of $C$, if for all elements $j_{1}$ of $J$, $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ConcreteCategory\_\_hom\_\_app} (\pi (j_{1}), c)$ is equal to $0$, then $c$ is equal to $0$. Let $x$ and $y$ be elements of the action of $Z$ and $C$ such that for all elements $j$ of $J$, $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ConcreteCategory\_\_hom\_\_app}$ applied to the map of $Z$ and $\pi (j)$ and $x$ is equal to $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ConcreteCategory\_\_hom\_\_app}$ applied to the map of $Z$ and $\pi (j)$ and $y$. Then $x$ is equal to $y$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎

Structure 67.213src/Lib/Cohomology/ProperBaseChange/ValuativeDensity.lean: ResiduePlace
Let $D$ and $P$ be categories of commutative rings. Let $\alpha$ be a homomorphism of $D$ and $P$. Then $\ResiduePlaceMACRoCOX {\alpha}$ is a set.
For α: D → P, a place: a prime q of P with a valuation ring O of the residue field κ(q) that contains the image of D.

Definition 67.258src/Lib/Cohomology/ProperBaseChange/ValuativeDensity.lean: KoszulExact
Let $A$ and $B$ be sets. Let $i$ be a function from $A$ to $B$. Let $g$ be a function from $B$ to $B$. Then $\KoszulExactMACRoBQI {i}{g}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that $i$ is injective, the fixed point of $g$ is a subset of the range of $i$ and $g - \mathrm{id}$ is surjective.
The map i is injective, every fixed point of g lies in its image, and z ↦ g(z) − z is onto.
Theorem 67.259src/Lib/Cohomology/ProperBaseChange/ValuativeDensity.lean: IsLine.of_acyclic

Theorem 67.269src/Lib/Cohomology/ProperBaseChange/ValuativeDensity.lean: perfectRing_monoidAlgebra

Theorem 67.271src/Lib/Cohomology/ProperBaseChange/ValuativeDensity.lean: laurent_split

Every Laurent polynomial over S is a polynomial in T plus a polynomial in T⁻¹ without constant term: S[T, T⁻¹] = S[T] + T⁻¹ S[T⁻¹], that is, H¹(ℙ¹, 𝒪) = 0.

Theorem 67.274src/Lib/Cohomology/ProperBaseChange/ValuativeDensity.lean: exists_perfection_quotient
Let $p \in \Natural$. Let $S$ and $B$ be commutative rings. Assume that $p$ is prime. Assume that $p$ is equal to $0$. Assume that $B$ is an algebra over $S$. Let $t \in B$. Then there exists an element $c$ of $\PerfectAlgMACRoCJW {S}$, such that there exists an algebra homomorphism $e$ of $B$ and the set of carriers of the object of $c$, such that for all elements $d$ of $\PerfectAlgMACRoCJW {S}$, for all algebra homomorphisms $\phi$ of $B$ and the set of carriers of the object of $d$, if $\phi (t)$ is equal to $0$, then there exists an algebra homomorphism $\psi$ of the set of carriers of the object of $c$ and the set of carriers of the object of $d$, such that the composition of $\psi$ and $e$ is equal to $\phi$, for all elements $r$ and $s$ of $B$, if $\EqtMACRoDE {e (r)}{e (s)}$, then there exists an element $n$ of $\Natural$, such that $t$ divides $r ^ {p ^ {n}}- s ^ {p ^ {n}}$, $e (t)$ is equal to $0$ and for all carriers $y$ of the object of $c$, there exists a natural number $n_{1}$, such that there exists an element $r_{1}$ of $B$, such that $e (r_{1})$ is equal to $y ^ {p ^ {n_{1}}}$.
The perfection of B/t, for p = 0 in S: a map e from B to a perfect S-algebra with e(t) = 0, through which every such map factors; e(r) = e(s) implies t ∣ r^{p^n} − s^{p^n} for some n, and every element has a p^n-th power in the image.

and 8 lemmas of Mathlib Ideal.mem_span_singleton', Function.bijective_of_subsingleton, Function.Surjective.exists, Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_dvd, iterate_frobenius, PerfectClosure.mk_eq_iff, Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ_surjective, Fact.out
. ∎

Theorem 67.293src/Lib/Cohomology/ProperBaseChange/ProperBaseChange.lean: IsProperOver.proper_base_change
Let $x$ be a prime natural number. Let $Y$ be a functor of the set of categories of commutative rings and the set of sets. Let $A$ be a commutative ring such that $\IsAICLocalMACRoBDQ {A}$ and $A$ is a valuation ring. Let $s$ be a homomorphism of $Y$ and $\reprByMACRoEQH {\mathrm{CommRingCat\_\_of} (A)}$ such that $\IsProperOverMACRoBMH {s}$. Assume that $x$ is a unit. Then $\ComponentsMACRoSH {\mathrm{AddCommGrpCat\_\_mk} (\ZModMACRoHB {x})}{\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Subfunctor\_\_\_u3b9\_} (\zeroPointsMACRoFBW {s}{\mathrm{IsLocalRing\_\_maximalIdeal} (A)})}$.
Proper base change: for a functor Y proper over an absolutely integrally closed valuation ring A and ℓ a prime invertible in A, restriction from Y to its closed fibre is bijective on all cohomology groups with coefficients ℤ/ℓ. Definition 67.294src/Lib/Cohomology/WoodsHole/IsolatedCoincidences.lean: ConstIfCoincident
Let $k$ be a set. Let $W$ and $X$ be functors of $\CommAlgMACRoFFA {k}$ and the set of sets. Let $u$ and $c$ be homomorphisms of $W$ and $X$. Let $x_{1}$ be an element of the action of $W$ and $\mathrm{CommAlgCat\_\_of}$ applied to $k$ and the set of dual numbers of $k$. Then $\ConstIfCoincidentMACRoSJ {u}{c}{x_{1}}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that if $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ConcreteCategory\_\_hom\_\_app}$ applied to the component of $c$ and $\mathrm{CommAlgCat\_\_of}$ applied to $k$ and the set of dual numbers of $k$ and $x_{1}$ is equal to $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ConcreteCategory\_\_hom\_\_app}$ applied to the component of $u$ and $\mathrm{CommAlgCat\_\_of}$ applied to $k$ and the set of dual numbers of $k$ and $x_{1}$, then $x_{1}$ is equal to $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ConcreteCategory\_\_hom\_\_app}$ applied to the map of $W$ and the composition of $\SqZeroExtfstMACRoCYX$ and $\structureMapMACRoEVD$ and $x_{1}$.
For a k[ε]-point x of W: if c(x) = u(x), then x is constant, that is, comes from the k-point below it.
Definition 67.295src/Lib/Cohomology/WoodsHole/IsolatedCoincidences.lean: IsCoincidenceSet

Theorem 67.296src/Lib/Cohomology/WoodsHole/IsolatedCoincidences.lean: exists_isCoincidenceSet

Theorem 67.299src/Lib/Cohomology/WoodsHole/IsolatedCoincidences.lean: IsTangentPoly.hasJacobianRatio
